--[[
    MAWWW HUB - AUTO FARMS
    Jalankan file ini dari executor. Tidak membutuhkan MAWWW_HUB_MAIN.lua.
    UI Auto Farms dibuat oleh script ini sendiri.
]]

local MAWWW_StartAutoFarms

-- Auto Farms starts directly; no key-system UI or verification gate.

MAWWW_StartAutoFarms = function()
local __afEnv = (type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv()) or _G

-- Bersihkan UI/worker lama jika file dieksekusi ulang.
if type(__afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_UI_CLEANUP) == "function" then
    pcall(__afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_UI_CLEANUP)
end

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
if not LocalPlayer then
    warn("[MAWWW Auto Farms] LocalPlayer tidak ditemukan. Jalankan saat sudah masuk game.")
    return
end

local __afGuiParent = nil
pcall(function()
    if type(gethui) == "function" then __afGuiParent = gethui() end
end)
if not __afGuiParent then
    pcall(function() __afGuiParent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
end
if not __afGuiParent then
    __afGuiParent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
end
local parent = __afGuiParent
local GUI_NAME = "MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_STANDALONE"

-- Isolasi runtime agar script ini tidak membutuhkan tabel/fungsi dari main hub.
local W = __afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_RUNTIME
if type(W) ~= "table" then W = {} end
__afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_RUNTIME = W
local VD = __afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_STATE
if type(VD) ~= "table" then VD = {} end
__afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_STATE = VD

local function ForceNotify(title, message, duration)
    local titleText = tostring(title or "MAWWW Auto Farms")
    local messageText = tostring(message or "")
    local ok = pcall(function()
        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = titleText,
            Text = messageText,
            Duration = tonumber(duration) or 3,
        })
    end)
    if not ok then
        warn("[MAWWW Auto Farms] " .. titleText .. ": " .. messageText)
    end
end
local VD_Notify = ForceNotify
--==============================================================
-- INVISIBLE MODE (logic diambil dari MAWWW_HUB_MAIN_NO_AUTOFARMS.lua)
-- Hanya fungsi mode; panel/GUI dari file sumber tidak disalin.
--==============================================================
VD.Invis_Enabled = VD.Invis_Enabled == true
VD.Invis_Hotkey = (type(VD.Invis_Hotkey) == "string" and VD.Invis_Hotkey ~= "" and VD.Invis_Hotkey) or "G"

local InvisMV = __afEnv.GlutoInvis
if type(InvisMV) ~= "table" then
    InvisMV = {
        Enabled = false, Loading = false, Ready = false, API = nil,
        _lastToggle = 0, _retries = 0, _retryMax = 3,
        _retryDelay = 2, _queueState = nil,
    }
    __afEnv.GlutoInvis = InvisMV
end
InvisMV.Enabled = InvisMV.Enabled == true
InvisMV.Loading = InvisMV.Loading == true
InvisMV.Ready = InvisMV.Ready == true
InvisMV._lastToggle = tonumber(InvisMV._lastToggle) or 0
InvisMV._retryMax = math.clamp(tonumber(InvisMV._retryMax) or 3, 1, 5)
InvisMV._retryDelay = math.clamp(tonumber(InvisMV._retryDelay) or 2, 0.5, 10)
VD.Invis_Enabled = InvisMV.Enabled

local INVIS_URL = "https://leekguy.vercel.app/roblox/menghub/crack_obf_invisible_93978595733734.lua"

local function Invis_ValidateAPI(api)
    return type(api) == "table"
        and type(api.enable) == "function"
        and type(api.disable) == "function"
end

local function Invis_Cleanup()
    pcall(function()
        local chair = Workspace:FindFirstChild("invischair")
        if chair then chair:Destroy() end
        local char = LocalPlayer.Character
        if char then
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") or part:IsA("Decal") then
                    if part.Name ~= "Hurtbox"
                        and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart"
                        and part.Name ~= "HRP_Clone" then
                        part.Transparency = 0
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 0
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function Invis_RefreshUI()
    local control = W.InvisibleUIToggle
    if control and type(control.Set) == "function" then
        pcall(control.Set, InvisMV.Enabled == true, false)
    end
end

local function Invis_LoadAPI()
    if Invis_ValidateAPI(InvisMV.API) then
        InvisMV.Ready = true
        InvisMV.Loading = false
        return true
    end
    if _G.MengHub and _G.MengHub.Invisible and Invis_ValidateAPI(_G.MengHub.Invisible) then
        InvisMV.API = _G.MengHub.Invisible
        InvisMV.Ready = true
        InvisMV.Loading = false
        return true
    end

    InvisMV.Loading = true
    InvisMV.Ready = false
    for attempt = 1, InvisMV._retryMax do
        InvisMV._retries = attempt
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local fetch = game:HttpGet(INVIS_URL)
            assert(type(fetch) == "string" and fetch ~= "", "Invisible API kosong")
            local compiler = loadstring
            assert(type(compiler) == "function", "loadstring tidak tersedia")
            local chunk, compileErr = compiler(fetch)
            assert(type(chunk) == "function", tostring(compileErr or "Gagal compile Invisible API"))
            chunk()
        end)
        task.wait(0.5)

        local api = _G.MengHub and _G.MengHub.Invisible
        if ok and Invis_ValidateAPI(api) then
            InvisMV.API = api
            InvisMV.Ready = true
            InvisMV.Loading = false
            if InvisMV._queueState ~= nil then
                local queued = InvisMV._queueState
                InvisMV._queueState = nil
                task.defer(function()
                    if W.Invisible_SetState then W.Invisible_SetState(queued, false, true) end
                end)
            end
            Invis_RefreshUI()
            return true
        end

        warn("[MAWWW Invisible] Percobaan " .. tostring(attempt) .. " gagal: " .. tostring(err or "API tidak ditemukan"))
        if attempt < InvisMV._retryMax then task.wait(InvisMV._retryDelay) end
    end

    InvisMV.Loading = false
    InvisMV.Ready = false
    Invis_RefreshUI()
    warn("[MAWWW Invisible] API gagal dimuat setelah beberapa percobaan.")
    return false
end

function W.Invisible_SetState(state, fromButton, forceApply)
    state = state == true
    local now = tick()
    if not forceApply and now - InvisMV._lastToggle < 0.25 then return end

    if not Invis_ValidateAPI(InvisMV.API) then
        if InvisMV.Loading then
            InvisMV._queueState = state
        else
            InvisMV._queueState = state
            task.spawn(function()
                if Invis_LoadAPI() then
                    local queued = InvisMV._queueState
                    InvisMV._queueState = nil
                    if queued ~= nil then
                        task.defer(function() W.Invisible_SetState(queued, fromButton, true) end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        if not fromButton then
            VD_Notify("Invisible", InvisMV.Loading and "Loading API..." or "Memuat ulang API...", 2)
        end
        Invis_RefreshUI()
        return
    end

    if InvisMV.Enabled == state then
        if not state then Invis_Cleanup() end
        VD.Invis_Enabled = state
        Invis_RefreshUI()
        return
    end

    InvisMV._lastToggle = now
    InvisMV.Enabled = state
    VD.Invis_Enabled = state
    if state then
        local ok, err = pcall(function() InvisMV.API.enable() end)
        if not ok then
            warn("[MAWWW Invisible] Gagal mengaktifkan: " .. tostring(err))
            InvisMV.Enabled = false
            VD.Invis_Enabled = false
            Invis_Cleanup()
            if not fromButton then VD_Notify("Invisible", "Gagal enable", 2) end
        elseif not fromButton then
            VD_Notify("Invisible", "Invisible AKTIF", 2)
        end
    else
        pcall(function() InvisMV.API.disable() end)
        Invis_Cleanup()
        if not fromButton then VD_Notify("Invisible", "Invisible Nonaktif", 2) end
    end
    Invis_RefreshUI()
end

function W.Invisible_SetEnabled(value) W.Invisible_SetState(value == true, false) end
function W.Invisible_Start() W.Invisible_SetState(true, false) end
function W.Invisible_Stop() W.Invisible_SetState(false, false) end
function W.Invisible_Toggle() W.Invisible_SetState(not InvisMV.Enabled, false) end
function W.Invisible_Restore() Invis_Cleanup() end
function W.Invisible_SetHotkey(value)
    if typeof(value) == "EnumItem" then
        VD.Invis_Hotkey = value.Name
    elseif type(value) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[value] then
        VD.Invis_Hotkey = value
    end
end
W.Invisible_IsReady = function() return InvisMV.Ready == true and Invis_ValidateAPI(InvisMV.API) end
W.Invisible_IsLoading = function() return InvisMV.Loading == true end
W.Invisible_IsOn = function() return InvisMV.Enabled == true end
W.Invisible_ReloadAPI = Invis_LoadAPI
W.Invisible_GetStatus = function()
    if InvisMV.Loading then return "LOADING" end
    if not W.Invisible_IsReady() then return "FAILED" end
    return InvisMV.Enabled and "ON" or "READY"
end

-- Hindari koneksi keyboard/character ganda saat script di-execute ulang.
for _, connectionName in ipairs({"InputConnection", "CharacterConnection", "RemovingConnection"}) do
    local oldConnection = InvisMV[connectionName]
    if oldConnection then pcall(function() oldConnection:Disconnect() end) end
    InvisMV[connectionName] = nil
end
InvisMV.InputConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed or input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
    local keyName = VD.Invis_Hotkey or "G"
    local keyCode = Enum.KeyCode[keyName]
    if keyCode and input.KeyCode == keyCode then
        W.Invisible_SetState(not InvisMV.Enabled, false)
    end
end)
InvisMV.CharacterConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.wait(1.2)
    if InvisMV.Enabled and Invis_ValidateAPI(InvisMV.API) then
        pcall(function() InvisMV.API.enable() end)
        Invis_RefreshUI()
    end
end)
InvisMV.RemovingConnection = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(leavingPlayer)
    if leavingPlayer == LocalPlayer then
        if InvisMV.Enabled and Invis_ValidateAPI(InvisMV.API) then
            pcall(function() InvisMV.API.disable() end)
        end
        Invis_Cleanup()
    end
end)
if not InvisMV.CloseHandlerBound then
    InvisMV.CloseHandlerBound = true
    pcall(function()
        game:BindToClose(function()
            if InvisMV.Enabled and Invis_ValidateAPI(InvisMV.API) then
                pcall(function() InvisMV.API.disable() end)
            end
            Invis_Cleanup()
        end)
    end)
end

task.spawn(function() Invis_LoadAPI() end)

local COLORS = {
    Background = Color3.fromRGB(15, 18, 28),
    Panel = Color3.fromRGB(23, 28, 42),
    Panel2 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 37, 55),
    Panel3 = Color3.fromRGB(39, 47, 68),
    Stroke = Color3.fromRGB(65, 82, 120),
    Text = Color3.fromRGB(239, 243, 255),
    SubText = Color3.fromRGB(160, 173, 199),
    Cyan = Color3.fromRGB(55, 190, 255),
    Green = Color3.fromRGB(75, 218, 145),
    Red = Color3.fromRGB(255, 92, 113),
}

local function new(className, properties, parentObject)
    local object = Instance.new(className)
    for key, value in pairs(properties or {}) do
        pcall(function() object[key] = value end)
    end
    if parentObject then object.Parent = parentObject end
    return object
end

local function corner(object, radius)
    return new("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 8) }, object)
end

local function stroke(object, color, thickness, transparency)
    return new("UIStroke", {
        Color = color or COLORS.Stroke,
        Thickness = thickness or 1,
        Transparency = transparency or 0,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
    }, object)
end

local function makeLabel(parentObject, text, textSize, position, size, font, color, zIndex)
    return new("TextLabel", {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Text = tostring(text or ""),
        TextSize = textSize or 12,
        Font = font or Enum.Font.Gotham,
        TextColor3 = color or COLORS.Text,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
        TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
        TextWrapped = true,
        Position = position or UDim2.new(),
        Size = size or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 20),
        ZIndex = zIndex or 10,
    }, parentObject)
end

        do
            -- Mawww Hub Auto Farm module using the supplied 1_clean.lua flow.
            -- This module uses the existing Mawww UI and does not create another UI library.
            local previousAF = W.N3K0_AutoFarm
            if type(previousAF) == "table" then
                previousAF.Alive = false
                previousAF.WebhookURL = ""
                if type(previousAF.Connections) == "table" then
                    for _, connection in ipairs(previousAF.Connections) do
                        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
                    end
                end
                if type(previousAF.Stop) == "function" then pcall(previousAF.Stop) end
            end

            -- Locked Auto Execute target. UI/config callbacks cannot override this URL.
            local AF_LOCKED_MAIN_SCRIPT_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mawww123/Mawwwhub/refs/heads/main/loader/auto-farms"
            local AF_DEFAULT_WEBHOOK_URL = "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/1440307769520689213/KpI_kG-I9mYxrbHP5xTBGXRr1p2aMSGaAfoJhZo-khGcWC1DXsz9pcxwznJPG-pm1l7R"

            local AF = {
                Alive = true,
                Status = "Idle",
                -- Source Auto Farm feature set, integrated into Mawww Hub's existing UI/runtime.
                AutoFarm = false, -- diaktifkan melalui toggle startup agar side-effect awal tetap dijalankan
                AutoEscape = false, -- compatibility alias for older config/callbacks
                RoundDetect = false,
                PlayerDetect = false,
                AutoHopAfterEscape = true,
                DisableKillerChance = true,
                HopIfKiller = true,
                InstantFinish = true,
                GateTweenSpeed = 100,
                MinPlayers = 2,
                MaxPlayers = 2,
                AutoExecuteOnHop = true,
                MainScriptURL = AF_LOCKED_MAIN_SCRIPT_URL,
                AntiAFK = true,
                NoClip = false, -- diaktifkan melalui toggle startup agar collision langsung diterapkan
                RoundHandled = false,
                CurrentRole = "Unknown",
                EscapeBusy = false,
                Hopping = false,
                LastTargetServerId = nil,
                RecentServers = {}, -- session-level blacklist also works when file APIs are unavailable
                KillerHopCooldownUntil = 0,
                NextEscapeAt = 0,
                NextHopAt = 0,
                ActiveTween = nil,
                NoClipOriginal = {},
                QueuePrepared = false,
                AwardWebhookEnabled = true,
                Connections = {},
                StatsBaseline = {},
                WebhookURL = AF_DEFAULT_WEBHOOK_URL, -- default supplied by the user; not written to config
            }
            W.N3K0_AutoFarm = AF
            VD.AF_Enabled = false
            AF.ServerHop = false -- compatibility alias for older UI callbacks

            local AF_TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
            local AF_HttpService = HttpService

            local function AF_Connect(signal, callback)
                if signal then
                    local ok, connection = pcall(function() return signal:Connect(callback) end)
                    if ok and connection then table.insert(AF.Connections, connection) end
                end
            end

            local function AF_IsValidWebhookURL(value)
                if type(value) ~= "string" then return false end
                return value:match("^https://discord%.com/api/webhooks/%d+/[%w_-]+$") ~= nil
            end

            local function AF_GetRequestFunction()
                local req
                pcall(function() if type(request) == "function" then req = request end end)
                if req then return req end
                pcall(function() if type(http_request) == "function" then req = http_request end end)
                if req then return req end
                pcall(function() if type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.request) == "function" then req = syn.request end end)
                if req then return req end
                pcall(function() if type(http) == "table" and type(http.request) == "function" then req = http.request end end)
                if req then return req end
                pcall(function() if type(fluxus) == "table" and type(fluxus.request) == "function" then req = fluxus.request end end)
                if req then return req end
                pcall(function()
                    local env = type(getgenv) == "function" and getgenv() or nil
                    if type(env) == "table" then
                        if type(env.request) == "function" then req = env.request
                        elseif type(env.http_request) == "function" then req = env.http_request end
                    end
                end)
                return req
            end

            local function AF_SendPayload(payload, showResult)
                local hookURL = tostring(AF.WebhookURL or "")
                if not AF_IsValidWebhookURL(hookURL) then
                    if showResult then ForceNotify("Award Webhook", "Masukkan URL webhook Discord baru yang valid.", 3) end
                    return false, "invalid webhook URL"
                end
                local okEncode, body = pcall(function() return AF_HttpService:JSONEncode(payload) end)
                if not okEncode or type(body) ~= "string" then
                    if showResult then ForceNotify("Award Webhook", "Gagal menyiapkan JSON webhook.", 3) end
                    return false, "json encode failed"
                end
                task.spawn(function()
                    local req = AF_GetRequestFunction()
                    local okRequest, response = pcall(function()
                        local requestData = {
                            Url = hookURL,
                            Method = "POST",
                            Headers = { ["Content-Type"] = "application/json" },
                            Body = body,
                        }
                        if req then return req(requestData) end
                        return AF_HttpService:RequestAsync(requestData)
                    end)
                    local statusCode = okRequest and type(response) == "table" and tonumber(response.StatusCode or response.Status) or nil
                    local success = okRequest and type(response) == "table" and (
                        response.Success == true or (statusCode and statusCode >= 200 and statusCode < 300)
                    )
                    if success then
                        if showResult then ForceNotify("Award Webhook", "Webhook berhasil dikirim.", 3) end
                    else
                        local reason = okRequest and ("HTTP status " .. tostring(statusCode or "unknown")) or tostring(response)
                        warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm Webhook] Request failed: " .. reason)
                        if showResult then ForceNotify("Award Webhook", "Gagal mengirim webhook. Periksa URL dan dukungan HTTP executor.", 4) end
                    end
                end)
                return true, "queued"
            end

            function AF.SendWebhook(eventTitle, details, forceSend, showResult)
                -- All Discord reporting is reserved for AF_SendAwardStats after a confirmed Escape Gate.
                return false, "Webhook hanya aktif untuk statistik setelah Escape Gate berhasil."
            end

            -- Statistics in this game are not guaranteed to be Player attributes.
            -- Resolve them from common Value objects / leaderstats / player data folders as well.
            local AF_STAT_ALIASES = {
                Screws = { "screws", "screw", "screwcount", "screwscount", "screwsvalue" },
                EXP = { "exp", "xp", "experience", "experiencepoints", "exppoints", "currentexp", "totalexp", "currentxp", "totalxp" },
                Level = { "level", "lvl", "playerlevel", "currentlevel" },
                Gears = { "gears", "gear", "gearcount", "gearscount", "gearsvalue" },
            }

            local function AF_NormalizeStatName(value)
                return tostring(value or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")
            end

            local function AF_ParseStatNumber(value)
                if type(value) == "number" then return value end
                if type(value) ~= "string" then return nil end
                local cleaned = value:gsub(",", ""):gsub("%s+", "")
                local numberPart, suffix = cleaned:match("^([%+%-]?%d*%.?%d+)([kKmMbB]?)$")
                if not numberPart then numberPart = cleaned:match("[-+]?%d*%.?%d+") end
                local number = tonumber(numberPart)
                if not number then return nil end
                suffix = (suffix or ""):lower()
                if suffix == "k" then number = number * 1000
                elseif suffix == "m" then number = number * 1000000
                elseif suffix == "b" then number = number * 1000000000 end
                return number
            end

            local function AF_NameMatchesStat(name, aliases)
                local normalized = AF_NormalizeStatName(name)
                for _, alias in ipairs(aliases or {}) do
                    if normalized == alias
                        or normalized == alias .. "value"
                        or normalized == alias .. "count"
                        or normalized == alias .. "amount"
                        or normalized == alias .. "stat"
                        or normalized == alias .. "label"
                        or normalized == alias .. "text" then
                        return true
                    end
                end
                return false
            end

            local function AF_ReadStatFromRoot(root, aliases)
                if not root then return nil end
                local function readObject(obj)
                    if not AF_NameMatchesStat(obj.Name, aliases) then return nil end
                    local ok, raw = pcall(function()
                        if obj:IsA("ValueBase") then return obj.Value end
                        if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") then return obj.Text end
                        return nil
                    end)
                    if ok then return AF_ParseStatNumber(raw) end
                    return nil
                end
                local result = readObject(root)
                if result ~= nil then return result end
                local ok, descendants = pcall(function() return root:GetDescendants() end)
                if not ok or type(descendants) ~= "table" then return nil end
                for _, obj in ipairs(descendants) do
                    result = readObject(obj)
                    if result ~= nil then return result end
                end
                return nil
            end

            local function AF_ReadAttribute(name)
                local aliases = AF_STAT_ALIASES[name] or { AF_NormalizeStatName(name) }
                local aliasSet = {}
                for _, alias in ipairs(aliases) do aliasSet[alias] = true end

                -- 1) Attributes, using aliases because the live game does not always use EXP.
                local okAttrs, attrs = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:GetAttributes() end)
                if okAttrs and type(attrs) == "table" then
                    for attrName, attrValue in pairs(attrs) do
                        if aliasSet[AF_NormalizeStatName(attrName)] then
                            local value = AF_ParseStatNumber(attrValue)
                            if value ~= nil then return value end
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- 2) Prefer the common stats containers on the player.
                local roots, seen = {}, {}
                local function addRoot(root)
                    if root and not seen[root] then seen[root] = true; table.insert(roots, root) end
                end
                for _, rootName in ipairs({ "leaderstats", "Stats", "stats", "Data", "data", "PlayerData", "PlayerStats", "Values", "Currencies" }) do
                    addRoot(LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild(rootName))
                end
                for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
                    local value = AF_ReadStatFromRoot(root, aliases)
                    if value ~= nil then return value end
                end

                -- 3) Some games place numeric values directly below Player or in PlayerGui.
                local value = AF_ReadStatFromRoot(LocalPlayer, aliases)
                if value ~= nil then return value end
                local playerGui
                pcall(function() playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") end)
                value = AF_ReadStatFromRoot(playerGui, aliases)
                if value ~= nil then return value end

                -- 4) Check player-keyed replicated data folders without scanning all replicated instances.
                for _, folderName in ipairs({ "PlayerData", "PlayerStats", "Stats", "Data", "Profiles" }) do
                    local folder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(folderName)
                    if folder then
                        local keyed = folder:FindFirstChild(tostring(LocalPlayer.UserId))
                            or folder:FindFirstChild(LocalPlayer.Name)
                        value = AF_ReadStatFromRoot(keyed, aliases)
                        if value ~= nil then return value end
                    end
                end
                return nil
            end

            local function AF_ReadStatsSnapshot()
                return {
                    Screws = AF_ReadAttribute("Screws"),
                    EXP = AF_ReadAttribute("EXP"),
                    Level = AF_ReadAttribute("Level"),
                    Gears = AF_ReadAttribute("Gears"),
                }
            end

            local function AF_CaptureStats(force)
                -- Freeze the round-start baseline once escape begins so endscreen/award events
                -- cannot overwrite it right before the webhook calculates the earned amounts.
                if AF.EscapeBusy and not force then return end
                local current = AF_ReadStatsSnapshot()
                AF.StatsBaseline = AF.StatsBaseline or {}
                -- Do not replace a real baseline with zero just because the stat has not replicated yet.
                for key, value in pairs(current) do
                    if value ~= nil then AF.StatsBaseline[key] = value end
                end
            end
            AF_CaptureStats()

            local function AF_FormatStat(value)
                if value == nil then return "N/A (stat belum ditemukan)" end
                if value == math.floor(value) then return tostring(math.floor(value)) end
                return tostring(value)
            end

            local function AF_FormatEarned(current, baseline)
                if current == nil or baseline == nil then return "N/A (data stat tidak lengkap)" end
                local earned = current - baseline
                -- Counters may reset between rounds; in that case current is the best available total.
                if earned < 0 then earned = current end
                return AF_FormatStat(earned)
            end

            local function AF_SendAwardStats()
                if not AF.AwardWebhookEnabled then return false end
                local base = AF.StatsBaseline or {}
                local current = AF_ReadStatsSnapshot()

                -- Give every tracked stat a chance to replicate. Do not stop as soon as
                -- only one counter changes (for example, Screws before EXP), or EXP can be stale.
                local waitUntil = tick() + 2
                while tick() < waitUntil do
                    local comparable, allComparableUpdated = 0, true
                    for _, key in ipairs({ "Screws", "EXP", "Gears", "Level" }) do
                        if base[key] ~= nil then
                            comparable = comparable + 1
                            if current[key] == nil or current[key] == base[key] then
                                allComparableUpdated = false
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if comparable > 0 and allComparableUpdated then break end
                    task.wait(0.35)
                    current = AF_ReadStatsSnapshot()
                end

                local levelBefore = base.Level ~= nil and AF_FormatStat(base.Level) or "N/A"
                local levelNow = current.Level ~= nil and AF_FormatStat(current.Level) or "N/A (stat belum ditemukan)"
                local payload = {
                    allowed_mentions = { parse = {} },
                    embeds = {{
                        title = "STATISTIK AUTO FARMS MAWWW",
                        color = 0x3399FF,
                        fields = {
                            { name = "🔩 Screws Earned", value = "```" .. AF_FormatEarned(current.Screws, base.Screws) .. "```", inline = true },
                            { name = "⚙️ Gears Earned", value = "```" .. AF_FormatEarned(current.Gears, base.Gears) .. "```", inline = true },
                            { name = "📈 Level Status", value = "```Level " .. levelBefore .. " ➜ Level " .. levelNow .. "```", inline = false },
                            { name = "✨ EXP Earned", value = "```" .. AF_FormatEarned(current.EXP, base.EXP) .. "```", inline = true },
                            { name = "EXP Total", value = "```" .. AF_FormatStat(current.EXP) .. "```", inline = true },
                        },
                        timestamp = os.date("!%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
                    }},
                }
                local sent = AF_SendPayload(payload, false)
                -- Update the next baseline only after this report has been assembled.
                AF_CaptureStats(true)
                return sent
            end

            -- GanKunZ-style Auto Farm flow, adapted for Mawww Hub.
            -- Map scans are cached/throttled to avoid continuous full-world scans on mobile.
            local AF_FinishPartCache = nil
            local AF_FinishTouchCache = {}
            local AF_MapCacheRef = nil
            local AF_NextMapScanAt = 0
            local AF_MapScanInterval = 4
            local AF_LobbyCacheAt = 0
            local AF_LobbyCacheValue = false
            local AF_LobbyCacheText = "Unknown"
            local AF_StatusNames = {
                "Status", "State", "GameState", "RoundStatus", "GameStatus",
                "Timer", "RoundTime", "Intermission", "Round", "Match", "GameValues",
            }

            local function AF_ContainsAny(text, fragments)
                local lower = string.lower(tostring(text or ""))
                for _, fragment in ipairs(fragments) do
                    if string.find(lower, fragment, 1, true) then return true end
                end
                return false
            end

            local function AF_NormalizeWorldName(name)
                return string.lower(tostring(name or "")):gsub("[%s_%-]", "")
            end

            local function AF_IsFinishName(name)
                local normalized = AF_NormalizeWorldName(name)
                -- Maps use several naming conventions for the survivor exit marker.
                -- Prefer explicit finish/escape/exit markers over the generic static fallback.
                return normalized:find("finish", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized:find("fininsh", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized == "escape"
                    or normalized:find("escapegate", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized:find("escapeexit", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized:find("escapezone", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized:find("escapehitbox", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized:find("escapeline", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized == "exit"
                    or normalized:find("exitgate", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized:find("exitdoor", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized:find("gateexit", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or normalized:find("survivorexit", 1, true) ~= nil
            end

            local function AF_GetRoleName()
                local teamName = LocalPlayer.Team and tostring(LocalPlayer.Team.Name or "") or ""
                if AF_ContainsAny(teamName, {"survivor"}) then return "Survivor" end
                if AF_ContainsAny(teamName, {"killer"}) then return "Killer" end
                if AF_ContainsAny(teamName, {"spectator"}) then return "Spectator" end

                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if character then
                    local roleObject = character:FindFirstChild("Team") or character:FindFirstChild("Role")
                    if roleObject then
                        local roleValue = nil
                        pcall(function()
                            if roleObject:IsA("ValueBase") then roleValue = roleObject.Value end
                        end)
                        local roleText = tostring(roleValue or roleObject.Name or "")
                        if AF_ContainsAny(roleText, {"survivor"}) then return "Survivor" end
                        if AF_ContainsAny(roleText, {"killer"}) then return "Killer" end
                        if AF_ContainsAny(roleText, {"spectator"}) then return "Spectator" end
                    end
                end
                return "Unknown"
            end
            AF.GetRoleName = AF_GetRoleName

            local function AF_FindPartFromObject(obj)
                if not obj then return nil end
                if obj:IsA("BasePart") then return obj end
                if obj:IsA("Model") then
                    local primary = obj.PrimaryPart
                    if primary and primary:IsA("BasePart") then return primary end
                    return obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                end
                return nil
            end

            local function AF_GetFinishTarget(forceRefresh)
                local now = tick()
                local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
                local cacheValid = false
                if not forceRefresh and AF_FinishPartCache then
                    pcall(function()
                        cacheValid = AF_FinishPartCache.Parent ~= nil
                            and AF_FinishPartCache:IsDescendantOf(Workspace)
                            and AF_MapCacheRef == map
                    end)
                end
                if cacheValid then
                    return AF_FinishPartCache.Position, AF_FinishPartCache, AF_FinishTouchCache
                end
                if not forceRefresh and now < AF_NextMapScanAt and AF_MapCacheRef == map then
                    return nil, nil, AF_FinishTouchCache
                end
                AF_NextMapScanAt = now + AF_MapScanInterval
                AF_MapCacheRef = map
                AF_FinishPartCache = nil
                AF_FinishTouchCache = {}
                if not map then return nil, nil, AF_FinishTouchCache end

                local descendants = {}
                local okDesc, resultDesc = pcall(function() return map:GetDescendants() end)
                if okDesc and type(resultDesc) == "table" then descendants = resultDesc end

                for _, obj in ipairs(descendants) do
                    local lowerName = string.lower(tostring(obj.Name or ""))
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") and (
                        lowerName:find("finish", 1, true)
                        or lowerName:find("escape", 1, true)
                        or lowerName:find("line", 1, true)
                        or lowerName == "hitbox"
                    ) then
                        table.insert(AF_FinishTouchCache, obj)
                    end
                    if not AF_FinishPartCache and AF_IsFinishName(obj.Name) then
                        AF_FinishPartCache = AF_FindPartFromObject(obj)
                    end
                end

                if AF_FinishPartCache then
                    return AF_FinishPartCache.Position, AF_FinishPartCache, AF_FinishTouchCache
                end

                -- Preserve the supplied source's known map coordinates, including its generic
                -- fallback for maps whose exit marker is not exposed under a recognizable name.
                -- Escape success is verified separately before webhook reporting/server hopping.
                local fallbackPosition = nil
                if map:FindFirstChild("Rooftop") or map:FindFirstChild("RooftopHitbox") then
                    fallbackPosition = Vector3.new(1546.12, 152.21, -796.72)
                elseif map:FindFirstChild("churchbell") then
                    fallbackPosition = Vector3.new(760.98, -20.14, -78.48)
                elseif map:FindFirstChild("HooksMeat") then
                    fallbackPosition = Vector3.new(-947.9, 152.12, -7579.52)
                else
                    fallbackPosition = Vector3.new(3098.16, 454.04, -4918.74)
                end
                return fallbackPosition, nil, AF_FinishTouchCache
            end

            local function AF_GetFinishPart()
                local _, part = AF_GetFinishTarget(false)
                return part
            end
            AF.FindFinishPart = AF_GetFinishPart

            local function AF_IsLobby(forceRefresh)
                local now = tick()
                if not forceRefresh and now < AF_LobbyCacheAt then
                    return AF_LobbyCacheValue, AF_LobbyCacheText
                end
                AF_LobbyCacheAt = now + 3
                local isLobby, lobbyText = false, "In Match"

                for _, name in ipairs(AF_StatusNames) do
                    local candidate = nil
                    pcall(function() candidate = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(name, true) end)
                    if candidate then
                        local isValue = false
                        pcall(function()
                            isValue = candidate:IsA("StringValue") or candidate:IsA("IntValue")
                                or candidate:IsA("NumberValue")
                        end)
                        if isValue then
                            local value = ""
                            pcall(function() value = tostring(candidate.Value) end)
                            if AF_ContainsAny(value, {"intermission", "waiting", "starting", "lobby", "prepare"}) then
                                isLobby, lobbyText = true, value
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not isLobby then
                    local playerGui = nil
                    pcall(function() playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") end)
                    if playerGui then
                        local okGui, guiDesc = pcall(function() return playerGui:GetDescendants() end)
                        if okGui and type(guiDesc) == "table" then
                            for _, obj in ipairs(guiDesc) do
                                if obj:IsA("TextLabel") and obj.Visible then
                                    local text = tostring(obj.Text or "")
                                    if AF_ContainsAny(text, {"intermission", "starting in", "starts in", "game start", "round start", "waiting for"}) then
                                        isLobby, lobbyText = true, text
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not isLobby then
                    local lobby = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Lobby") or Workspace:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation")
                    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local lobbyPosition = nil
                    if lobby then
                        if lobby:IsA("BasePart") then
                            lobbyPosition = lobby.Position
                        else
                            local part = lobby:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                            if part then lobbyPosition = part.Position end
                        end
                    end
                    if root and lobbyPosition and (root.Position - lobbyPosition).Magnitude < 130 then
                        isLobby, lobbyText = true, "Masih di Lobby"
                    end
                end

                AF_LobbyCacheValue, AF_LobbyCacheText = isLobby, lobbyText
                return isLobby, lobbyText
            end

            local function AF_ReadBlacklist()
                local result = {}
                pcall(function()
                    if type(isfile) == "function" and isfile("ServerHop.txt") then
                        local raw = readfile("ServerHop.txt")
                        for line in string.gmatch(tostring(raw or ""), "[^\r\n]+") do
                            local id, stamp = line:match("^([^|]+)|(%d+)$")
                            stamp = tonumber(stamp)
                            if id and stamp and os.time() - stamp < 600 then result[id] = stamp end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                -- Keep recent target IDs in memory too. This prevents Delta builds without
                -- filesystem APIs from immediately selecting the same full server again.
                AF.RecentServers = AF.RecentServers or {}
                for id, stamp in pairs(AF.RecentServers) do
                    if tonumber(stamp) and os.time() - tonumber(stamp) < 600 then
                        result[tostring(id)] = tonumber(stamp)
                    else
                        AF.RecentServers[id] = nil
                    end
                end
                return result
            end

            local function AF_RecordServer(serverId)
                if not serverId then return end
                serverId = tostring(serverId)
                AF.RecentServers = AF.RecentServers or {}
                AF.RecentServers[serverId] = os.time()
                local history = AF_ReadBlacklist()
                history[serverId] = os.time()
                if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return end
                local rows = {}
                for id, stamp in pairs(history) do
                    table.insert(rows, tostring(id) .. "|" .. tostring(stamp))
                end
                pcall(function() writefile("ServerHop.txt", table.concat(rows, "\n")) end)
            end

            function AF.ResetServerBlacklist()
                local ok = false
                pcall(function()
                    if type(delfile) == "function" and type(isfile) == "function" and isfile("ServerHop.txt") then
                        delfile("ServerHop.txt")
                        ok = true
                    elseif type(writefile) == "function" then
                        writefile("ServerHop.txt", "")
                        ok = true
                    end
                end)
                AF.Status = ok and "Server hop blacklist reset" or "Executor does not support blacklist reset"
                ForceNotify("Auto Farm", AF.Status, 3)
                return ok
            end

            local function AF_FetchServerPage(cursor)
                local pageURL = string.format(
                    "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%s/servers/Public?limit=100&sortOrder=Asc&excludeFullGames=true",
                    tostring(game.PlaceId)
                )
                if cursor and cursor ~= "" then
                    local encodedCursor = cursor
                    pcall(function() encodedCursor = AF_HttpService:UrlEncode(cursor) end)
                    pageURL = pageURL .. "&cursor=" .. encodedCursor
                end
                local req = AF_GetRequestFunction()
                if req then
                    local requestOK, response = pcall(req, { Url = pageURL, Method = "GET" })
                    if requestOK and type(response) == "table" and response.Success ~= false
                        and type(response.Body) == "string" and response.Body ~= "" then
                        local decodeOK, decoded = pcall(function() return AF_HttpService:JSONDecode(response.Body) end)
                        if decodeOK and type(decoded) == "table" and type(decoded.data) == "table" then
                            return decoded
                        end
                    end
                end
                -- Delta builds may expose a request API that rejects this endpoint; try HttpGet safely.
                local httpOK, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(pageURL) end)
                if not httpOK or type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then
                    error("Server list request failed (request and HttpGet unavailable)")
                end
                local decodeOK, decoded = pcall(function() return AF_HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
                if not decodeOK or type(decoded) ~= "table" or type(decoded.data) ~= "table" then
                    error("Server list response was not valid JSON")
                end
                return decoded
            end

            local function AF_QueueMainOnTeleport(target, reason)
                if not AF.AutoExecuteOnHop then return true end
                -- Always use the locked, verified-by-owner URL; ignore environment/config overrides.
                local mainURL = AF_LOCKED_MAIN_SCRIPT_URL
                AF.MainScriptURL = AF_LOCKED_MAIN_SCRIPT_URL
                if type(mainURL) ~= "string" or not mainURL:match("^https://raw%.githubusercontent%.com/") then
                    ForceNotify("Auto Execute", "URL loader terkunci tidak valid.", 3)
                    return false
                end
                local queueFn = nil
                pcall(function()
                    queueFn = queue_on_teleport or queueonteleport
                    if not queueFn and type(getgenv) == "function" then
                        local env = getgenv()
                        if type(env) == "table" then
                            queueFn = env.queue_on_teleport or env.queueonteleport
                            if not queueFn and type(env.syn) == "table" then queueFn = env.syn.queue_on_teleport end
                            if not queueFn and type(env.fluxus) == "table" then queueFn = env.fluxus.queue_on_teleport end
                            if not queueFn and type(env.delta) == "table" then queueFn = env.delta.queue_on_teleport or env.delta.queueonteleport end
                            if not queueFn and type(env.Delta) == "table" then queueFn = env.Delta.queue_on_teleport or env.Delta.queueonteleport end
                        end
                    end
                    if not queueFn and type(syn) == "table" then queueFn = syn.queue_on_teleport end
                    if not queueFn and type(fluxus) == "table" then queueFn = fluxus.queue_on_teleport end
                end)
                if type(queueFn) ~= "function" then
                    ForceNotify("Auto Execute", "Executor tidak mendukung queue_on_teleport.", 3)
                    return false
                end
                local payload = "loadstring(game:HttpGet(" .. string.format("%q", mainURL) .. "))()"
                local okQueue, queueErr = pcall(queueFn, payload)
                if not okQueue then
                    warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] Auto execute queue failed: " .. tostring(queueErr))
                    return false
                end
                AF.QueuePrepared = true
                return true
            end

            function AF.HopNow(reason)
                if AF.Hopping or not AF.Alive then return false end
                local now = tick()
                if now < (AF.NextHopAt or 0) then return false end

                AF.Hopping = true
                AF.Status = "Searching public server..."
                local blacklist = AF_ReadBlacklist()
                local cursor = nil
                local target = nil
                local fallback = nil
                local minPlayers = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(AF.MinPlayers) or 2))
                local maxPlayers = math.max(minPlayers, math.floor(tonumber(AF.MaxPlayers) or 4))
                local pageCount = 0

                for pageIndex = 1, 8 do
                    if not AF.Alive then break end
                    pageCount = pageIndex
                    local okPage, data = pcall(AF_FetchServerPage, cursor)
                    if not okPage or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data.data) ~= "table" then
                        AF.Status = "Server list unavailable"
                        warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] Server list error: " .. tostring(data))
                        break
                    end
                    for _, server in ipairs(data.data) do
                        local id = tostring(server.id or "")
                        local playing = tonumber(server.playing)
                        local capacity = tonumber(server.maxPlayers)
                        if id ~= "" and id ~= game.JobId and not blacklist[id]
                            and playing and capacity and playing >= 0 and capacity > 0 and playing < capacity then
                            -- Prefer the least-populated server within the configured range;
                            -- server lists can become stale between lookup and teleport.
                            if playing >= minPlayers and playing <= maxPlayers then
                                if not target or playing < (tonumber(target.playing) or math.huge) then
                                    target = server
                                end
                            elseif playing >= 1 and playing < maxPlayers then
                                if not fallback or playing < (tonumber(fallback.playing) or math.huge) then
                                    fallback = server
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if target then break end
                    cursor = data.nextPageCursor
                    if type(cursor) ~= "string" or cursor == "" then break end
                    task.wait(0.15)
                end

                target = target or fallback
                if not target then
                    AF.Hopping = false
                    AF.NextHopAt = tick() + 12
                    AF.Status = AF.Status == "Server list unavailable" and AF.Status or "No open server in the selected range"
                    ForceNotify("Auto Farm", AF.Status .. " • akan mencoba lagi", 3)
                    if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm then
                        task.delay(12.5, function()
                            if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and not AF.Hopping then
                                AF.HopNow("Server search retry")
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    return false
                end

                AF.LastTargetServerId = tostring(target.id)
                local queueCallOK, queueResult = pcall(AF_QueueMainOnTeleport, target, reason)
                if not queueCallOK then
                    warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] Auto Execute queue helper error: " .. tostring(queueResult))
                end
                -- Mark target before teleport, so a server that has just filled is skipped on retry.
                AF_RecordServer(AF.LastTargetServerId)
                local okTeleport, teleportErr = pcall(function()
                    AF_TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, target.id, LocalPlayer)
                end)
                if not okTeleport then
                    local teleportErrorText = tostring(teleportErr or ""):lower()
                    local targetLooksFull = teleportErrorText:find("full", 1, true) ~= nil
                        or teleportErrorText:find("penuh", 1, true) ~= nil
                        or teleportErrorText:find("gamefull", 1, true) ~= nil
                    AF.Hopping = false
                    AF.EscapeBusy = false
                    local retryDelay = targetLooksFull and 1.5 or 5
                    AF.NextHopAt = tick() + retryDelay
                    AF.Status = targetLooksFull and "Target unavailable • finding another server" or "Teleport request failed; retry delayed"
                    warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] Teleport request failed: " .. tostring(teleportErr))
                    ForceNotify("Auto Farm", AF.Status, 3)
                    if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm then
                        task.delay(retryDelay, function()
                            if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and not AF.Hopping then
                                AF.HopNow(targetLooksFull and "Full server retry" or "Teleport request retry")
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    return false
                end

                AF.Status = "Teleporting to server with " .. tostring(target.playing) .. " players..."
                AF.NextHopAt = tick() + 35
                task.delay(25, function()
                    if AF.Alive and AF.Hopping then
                        AF.Hopping = false
                        AF.EscapeBusy = false
                        AF.QueuePrepared = false
                        AF.NextHopAt = math.max(AF.NextHopAt or 0, tick() + 10)
                        AF.Status = "Server hop timed out; waiting before retry"
                    end
                end)
                return true
            end

            local function AF_TouchFinish(root, targetPosition, candidates)
                if not root or not targetPosition then return end
                local touchFn = nil
                pcall(function()
                    touchFn = firetouchinterest
                    if type(touchFn) ~= "function" and type(getgenv) == "function" then
                        local env = getgenv()
                        if type(env) == "table" then touchFn = env.firetouchinterest end
                    end
                end)
                if type(touchFn) ~= "function" then return end
                local touched = 0
                for _, part in ipairs(candidates or {}) do
                    if touched >= 8 then break end
                    local okPart, usable = pcall(function()
                        return part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart")
                            and (part.Position - targetPosition).Magnitude < 35
                    end)
                    if okPart and usable then
                        local okTouch = pcall(function()
                            touchFn(root, part, 0)
                            task.wait(0.04)
                            touchFn(root, part, 1)
                        end)
                        if okTouch then touched = touched + 1 end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function AF_StartEscape()
                if not AF.AutoFarm or AF.EscapeBusy or AF.Hopping or AF.RoundHandled
                    or tick() < (AF.NextEscapeAt or 0) then return end
                local role = AF_GetRoleName()
                AF.CurrentRole = role

                if role == "Killer" then
                    if not AF.HopIfKiller or tick() < (AF.KillerHopCooldownUntil or 0) then
                        AF.Status = "Killer detected"
                        return
                    end
                    AF.EscapeBusy = true
                    AF.KillerHopCooldownUntil = tick() + 30
                    AF.Status = "Killer detected • preparing server hop"
                    task.delay(1.5, function()
                        if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and AF.HopIfKiller then
                            AF.HopNow("Killer detected")
                        else
                            AF.EscapeBusy = false
                        end
                    end)
                    task.delay(8, function()
                        if AF.Alive and not AF.Hopping then AF.EscapeBusy = false end
                    end)
                    return
                end

                if role ~= "Survivor" then
                    AF.Status = role == "Spectator" and "Waiting for next round" or "Waiting for Survivor/Killer role"
                    return
                end

                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                    AF.Status = "Waiting for character"
                    return
                end

                local targetPosition, finishPart, touchParts = AF_GetFinishTarget(false)
                if not targetPosition then
                    AF.Status = "Searching for finish line..."
                    return
                end

                AF.RoundHandled = true
                AF.EscapeBusy = true
                AF.NextEscapeAt = tick() + 25
                AF.Status = "Finish found • waiting 6 seconds"
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(6)
                    if not AF.Alive or not AF.AutoFarm or AF.Hopping then
                        AF.EscapeBusy = false
                        AF.RoundHandled = false
                        if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and not AF.Hopping then AF.NextEscapeAt = tick() + 3 end
                        return
                    end
                    local currentRole = AF_GetRoleName()
                    if currentRole ~= "Survivor" or not root.Parent or not humanoid.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
                        AF.Status = "Escape cancelled: role/character changed"
                        AF.EscapeBusy = false
                        AF.RoundHandled = false
                        AF.NextEscapeAt = tick() + 4
                        return
                    end

                    -- Re-resolve a moving/destroyed finish marker after the source delay.
                    local refreshedPosition, refreshedPart, refreshedTouches = AF_GetFinishTarget(true)
                    if refreshedPosition then targetPosition = refreshedPosition end
                    if refreshedPart then finishPart = refreshedPart end
                    if refreshedTouches then touchParts = refreshedTouches end
                    if not targetPosition then
                        AF.Status = "Finish line not found"
                        AF.EscapeBusy = false
                        AF.RoundHandled = false
                        AF.NextEscapeAt = tick() + 4
                        return
                    end

                    local destination = CFrame.new(targetPosition + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0))
                    local moved = false
                    local moveError = nil
                    if AF.InstantFinish then
                        local okMove, errMove = pcall(function() root.CFrame = destination end)
                        moved, moveError = okMove, errMove
                    else
                        local distance = (root.Position - targetPosition).Magnitude
                        local speed = math.max(tonumber(AF.GateTweenSpeed) or 100, 20)
                        local duration = math.clamp(distance / speed, 0.5, 4)
                        local okTween, tweenOrError = pcall(function()
                            return TweenService:Create(root, TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { CFrame = destination })
                        end)
                        if okTween and tweenOrError then
                            AF.ActiveTween = tweenOrError
                            local playOk, playErr = pcall(function() tweenOrError:Play() end)
                            if playOk then
                                task.wait(duration + 0.1)
                                moved = AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and root.Parent ~= nil
                                if AF.ActiveTween == tweenOrError then AF.ActiveTween = nil end
                                pcall(function() tweenOrError:Cancel() end)
                            else
                                moveError = playErr
                                pcall(function() tweenOrError:Cancel() end)
                            end
                        else
                            moveError = tweenOrError
                        end
                    end

                    if not moved then
                        AF.Status = "Auto Escape failed • retry scheduled"
                        AF.EscapeBusy = false
                        AF.RoundHandled = false
                        AF.NextEscapeAt = tick() + 4
                        if moveError then warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] Move failed: " .. tostring(moveError)) end
                        return
                    end

                    -- Reaching the coordinates is not proof that the game awarded the escape.
                    -- Touch nearby gate parts, then wait for the game to mark this player Spectator
                    -- before sending award stats or hopping. This avoids losing EXP/reward replication.
                    AF_TouchFinish(root, targetPosition, touchParts)
                    AF.Status = "At exit • waiting for Spectator confirmation"
                    local spectatorConfirmed = false
                    local spectatorDeadline = tick() + 35
                    while AF.Alive and tick() < spectatorDeadline do
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        if AF_GetRoleName() == "Spectator" then
                            spectatorConfirmed = true
                            break
                        end
                        if not root.Parent then break end
                    end

                    if not AF.Alive then
                        AF.EscapeBusy = false
                        return
                    end

                    if not spectatorConfirmed then
                        AF.Status = "Escape not confirmed • server hop cancelled"
                        AF.EscapeBusy = false
                        AF.RoundHandled = false
                        AF.NextEscapeAt = tick() + 8
                        warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] Spectator confirmation timed out; skipping server hop to preserve rewards.")
                        return
                    end

                    AF.Status = "Spectator confirmed • collecting award stats"
                    AF_SendAwardStats()

                    if AF.AutoHopAfterEscape and AF.AutoFarm and not AF.Hopping then
                        -- AF_SendAwardStats waits briefly for the reward values to replicate.
                        -- Give that report a little more time to queue before changing servers.
                        task.wait(1.5)
                        if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and not AF.Hopping then
                            local hopStarted = AF.HopNow("Escape confirmed")
                            if not hopStarted then
                                AF.EscapeBusy = false
                                AF.Status = "Escape confirmed • server hop retry delayed"
                                task.delay(12.5, function()
                                    if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and not AF.Hopping then
                                        AF.HopNow("Escape confirmed retry")
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        AF.Status = "Round completed • waiting next round"
                        AF.EscapeBusy = false
                    end
                end)
            end

            local function AF_RestoreNoClip()
                for part, original in pairs(AF.NoClipOriginal or {}) do
                    pcall(function()
                        if part and part.Parent and part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = original end
                    end)
                end
                AF.NoClipOriginal = {}
            end

            local function AF_ApplyNoClipOnce()
                if not AF.NoClip then return end
                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if not character then return end
                for _, part in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide then
                        if AF.NoClipOriginal[part] == nil then AF.NoClipOriginal[part] = true end
                        pcall(function() part.CanCollide = false end)
                    end
                end
            end

            local function AF_SetNoClip(value)
                AF.NoClip = value == true
                if AF.NoClip then
                    AF_ApplyNoClipOnce()
                else
                    AF_RestoreNoClip()
                end
            end

            function AF.SetAutoFarm(value)
                local enabled = value == true
                if AF.AutoFarm == enabled then return end
                AF.AutoFarm = enabled
                AF.AutoEscape = enabled -- legacy state alias
                VD.AF_Enabled = enabled
                AF.Status = enabled and "Auto Farm enabled" or "Auto Farm disabled"
                if not enabled then
                    AF.RoundHandled = false
                    AF.EscapeBusy = false
                    if AF.ActiveTween then pcall(function() AF.ActiveTween:Cancel() end); AF.ActiveTween = nil end
                else
                    AF.NextEscapeAt = 0
                    AF_CaptureStats(true)
                end
            end
            function AF.SetAutoEscape(value) AF.SetAutoFarm(value) end
            function AF.SetAutoHopAfterEscape(value) AF.AutoHopAfterEscape = value == true end
            function AF.SetDisableKillerChance(value) AF.DisableKillerChance = value == true end
            function AF.SetHopIfKiller(value) AF.HopIfKiller = value == true end
            function AF.SetInstantFinish(value) AF.InstantFinish = value == true end
            function AF.SetGateTweenSpeed(value) AF.GateTweenSpeed = math.clamp(tonumber(value) or 100, 20, 300) end
            function AF.SetMinPlayers(value)
                AF.MinPlayers = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(value) or 2), 1, 8)
                if AF.MaxPlayers < AF.MinPlayers then AF.MaxPlayers = AF.MinPlayers end
            end
            function AF.SetMaxPlayers(value)
                AF.MaxPlayers = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(value) or 4), 1, 8)
                if AF.MinPlayers > AF.MaxPlayers then AF.MinPlayers = AF.MaxPlayers end
            end
            function AF.SetAutoExecuteOnHop(value) AF.AutoExecuteOnHop = value == true end
            function AF.SetMainScriptURL(_value)
                AF.MainScriptURL = AF_LOCKED_MAIN_SCRIPT_URL
                return AF.MainScriptURL
            end
            function AF.SetAntiAFK(value) AF.AntiAFK = value == true end
            function AF.SetNoClip(value) AF_SetNoClip(value) end
            function AF.SetAwardWebhook(value) AF.AwardWebhookEnabled = value == true end

            -- Compatibility aliases for older config IDs/callbacks that may still be present.
            function AF.SetRoundDetect(value) AF.RoundDetect = value == true end
            function AF.SetPlayerDetect(value) AF.PlayerDetect = value == true; AF.ServerHop = AF.PlayerDetect end
            AF.SetEnabled = AF.SetAutoFarm
            AF.SetServerHop = AF.SetPlayerDetect
            AF.Start = function() AF.SetAutoFarm(true) end
            AF.Stop = function()
                AF.SetAutoFarm(false)
                AF.RoundDetect = false
                AF.PlayerDetect = false
                AF_SetNoClip(false)
                AF.AntiAFK = false
            end
            AF.ServerHop = false

            local remotesRoot = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local statusUpdateEvent = remotesRoot and remotesRoot:FindFirstChild("StatusUpdateEvent")
            local timeUpdateEvent = remotesRoot and remotesRoot:FindFirstChild("TimeUpdateEvent")
            local function AF_ResetRoundState()
                -- Ignore round signals that arrive while a finish teleport/report is in progress.
                -- This prevents a duplicate escape loop if the game emits more than one state event.
                if AF.EscapeBusy or AF.Hopping then return end
                AF.RoundHandled = false
                AF.NextEscapeAt = 0
                task.delay(0.25, function()
                    if AF.Alive and not AF.EscapeBusy then AF_CaptureStats() end
                end)
            end

            AF_Connect(statusUpdateEvent and statusUpdateEvent.OnClientEvent, function(argument)
                local eventText = tostring(argument or "")
                if AF_ContainsAny(eventText, {"intermissionstarting", "waitingforplayers", "intermission", "roundstart", "matchstart"}) then
                    AF_ResetRoundState()
                end
            end)
            AF_Connect(timeUpdateEvent and timeUpdateEvent.OnClientEvent, function(argument)
                local eventText = tostring(argument or "")
                if eventText == "Round" or eventText == "Starting" or AF_ContainsAny(eventText, {"intermission", "waitingforplayers"}) then
                    AF_ResetRoundState()
                end
                -- This check must not be an elseif: "Round" is already handled above.
                if AF.RoundDetect and eventText == "Round" and AF_GetRoleName() == "Spectator" then
                    task.spawn(function() AF.HopNow("Round detect") end)
                end
            end)
            AF_Connect(AF_TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed, function(who, result, message)
                if who ~= LocalPlayer then return end
                local failureText = (tostring(message or "") .. " " .. tostring(result or "")):lower()
                local looksFull = failureText:find("full", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or failureText:find("penuh", 1, true) ~= nil
                    or failureText:find("gamefull", 1, true) ~= nil
                if AF.LastTargetServerId then
                    AF_RecordServer(AF.LastTargetServerId)
                end
                AF.Hopping = false
                AF.EscapeBusy = false
                AF.QueuePrepared = false
                local retryDelay = looksFull and 1.5 or 5
                AF.NextHopAt = tick() + retryDelay
                AF.Status = looksFull and "Target server changed/full • looking for another" or "Teleport failed; retry delayed"
                warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] Teleport failed: " .. tostring(message or result))
                if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm then
                    task.delay(retryDelay, function()
                        if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and not AF.Hopping then
                            AF.HopNow(looksFull and "Full server retry" or "Teleport retry")
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            AF_Connect(LocalPlayer.Idled, function()
                if not AF.Alive or not AF.AntiAFK then return end
                pcall(function()
                    local virtualUser = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
                    local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
                    virtualUser:CaptureController()
                    virtualUser:Button2Down(Vector2.new(0, 0), camera and camera.CFrame or CFrame.new())
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    virtualUser:Button2Up(Vector2.new(0, 0), camera and camera.CFrame or CFrame.new())
                end)
            end)

            -- Separate low-frequency workers: no per-frame map scan and no busy idle loops.
            task.spawn(function()
                while AF.Alive and W.N3K0_AutoFarm == AF do
                    task.wait(AF.AutoFarm and 0.75 or 4)
                    if AF.Alive and AF.AutoFarm and not AF.Hopping then
                        local okStep, stepErr = pcall(function()
                            local isLobby, lobbyText = AF_IsLobby(false)
                            if isLobby then
                                AF.RoundHandled = false
                                if not AF.EscapeBusy then AF.NextEscapeAt = 0 end
                                AF.Status = "Waiting for match (" .. tostring(lobbyText) .. ")"
                                return
                            end
                            AF_StartEscape()
                        end)
                        if not okStep then
                            AF.Status = "Auto Farm error"
                            warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] Step error: " .. tostring(stepErr))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                while AF.Alive and W.N3K0_AutoFarm == AF do
                    task.wait(AF.DisableKillerChance and 10 or 5)
                    if AF.Alive and AF.DisableKillerChance then
                        pcall(function()
                            local root = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes") or ReplicatedStorage
                            local remote = root:FindFirstChild("SetKillerChance") or root:FindFirstChild("KillerChance")
                            if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then remote:FireServer(0) end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                while AF.Alive and W.N3K0_AutoFarm == AF do
                    task.wait(AF.NoClip and 1.0 or 4)
                    if AF.Alive and AF.NoClip then
                        local okClip, errClip = pcall(AF_ApplyNoClipOnce)
                        if not okClip then warn("[MAWWW Auto Farm] NoClip error: " .. tostring(errClip)) end
                    end
                end
                AF_RestoreNoClip()
            end)

            AF_Connect(LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded, function(character)
                AF.NoClipOriginal = {}
                if AF.NoClip then task.delay(0.35, function() if AF.Alive and AF.NoClip then AF_ApplyNoClipOnce() end end) end
            end)
        end



local LOGO = "rbxassetid://116986879010501"

    -- so the icon does not depend on a stale or incomplete local image asset.
    local MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_IMAGE = LOGO
    local MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_CLOSED_IMAGE = LOGO

    local MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_CLOSED_IMAGE = nil

            assetGetter = getsynasset
