        local function decodeAndLoadFloatingImage(fileName, encodedImage)
            local alphabet = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
            local lookup = {}
            for i = 1, #alphabet do
                lookup[string.sub(alphabet, i, i)] = i - 1
            end

            local output = {}
            local buffer, bitCount = 0, 0
            for i = 1, #encodedImage do
                local char = string.sub(encodedImage, i, i)
                if char == "=" then break end
                local value = lookup[char]
                if value ~= nil then
                    buffer = buffer * 64 + value
                    bitCount = bitCount + 6
                    if bitCount >= 8 then
                        bitCount = bitCount - 8
                        output[#output + 1] = string.char(math.floor(buffer / (2 ^ bitCount)) % 256)
                        buffer = buffer % (2 ^ bitCount)
                    end
                end
            end

            writefile(fileName, table.concat(output))
            return assetGetter(fileName)
        end

        if type(writefile) == "function" and type(assetGetter) == "function" then
            local headerLogoOk, headerLogoUri = pcall(function()
                return decodeAndLoadFloatingImage(
                    "mawww_hub_header_logo.jpg",
                    MAWWW_HEADER_LOGO_BASE64
                )
            end)
            if headerLogoOk and type(headerLogoUri) == "string" and headerLogoUri ~= "" then
                MAWWW_HEADER_LOGO_IMAGE = headerLogoUri
            end

            local openOk, openUri = pcall(function()
                return decodeAndLoadFloatingImage(
                    "mawww_hub_mawww_catgirl_floating_icon_open.png",
                    MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_OPEN_BASE64
                )
            end)
            if openOk and type(openUri) == "string" and openUri ~= "" then
                MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_IMAGE = openUri
            end

            local closedOk, closedUri = pcall(function()
                return decodeAndLoadFloatingImage(
                    "mawww_hub_mawww_catgirl_floating_icon_closed.png",
                    MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_CLOSED_BASE64
                )
            end)
            if closedOk and type(closedUri) == "string" and closedUri ~= "" then
                MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_CLOSED_IMAGE = closedUri
            else
                MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_CLOSED_IMAGE = MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_IMAGE
            end

            local headOpenOk, headOpenUri = pcall(function()
                return decodeAndLoadFloatingImage(
                    "mawww_hub_catgirl_head_motion_open.png",
                    MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_OPEN_BASE64
                )
            end)
            if headOpenOk and type(headOpenUri) == "string" and headOpenUri ~= "" then
                MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_OPEN_IMAGE = headOpenUri
            end

            local headClosedOk, headClosedUri = pcall(function()
                return decodeAndLoadFloatingImage(
                    "mawww_hub_catgirl_head_motion_closed.png",
                    MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_CLOSED_BASE64
                )
            end)
            if headClosedOk and type(headClosedUri) == "string" and headClosedUri ~= "" then
                MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_CLOSED_IMAGE = headClosedUri
            else
                MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_CLOSED_IMAGE = MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_OPEN_IMAGE
            end

            local hairOk, hairUri = pcall(function()
                return decodeAndLoadFloatingImage(
                    "mawww_hub_catgirl_hair_motion_layer.png",
                    MAWWW_FLOATING_HAIR_LAYER_BASE64
                )
            end)
            if hairOk and type(hairUri) == "string" and hairUri ~= "" then
                MAWWW_FLOATING_HAIR_LAYER_IMAGE = hairUri
            end

            local bodyOk, bodyUri = pcall(function()
                return decodeAndLoadFloatingImage(
                    "mawww_hub_catgirl_body_motion_layer.png",
                    MAWWW_FLOATING_BODY_LAYER_BASE64
                )
            end)
            if bodyOk and type(bodyUri) == "string" and bodyUri ~= "" then
                MAWWW_FLOATING_BODY_LAYER_IMAGE = bodyUri
            end

            -- Do not show an inpainted backing frame without its matching layers.
            if not (openOk and closedOk and headOpenOk and headClosedOk and hairOk and bodyOk
                and type(openUri) == "string" and openUri ~= ""
                and type(closedUri) == "string" and closedUri ~= ""
                and type(headOpenUri) == "string" and headOpenUri ~= ""
                and type(headClosedUri) == "string" and headClosedUri ~= ""
                and type(hairUri) == "string" and hairUri ~= ""
                and type(bodyUri) == "string" and bodyUri ~= "") then
                MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_IMAGE = LOGO
                MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_CLOSED_IMAGE = LOGO
                MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_OPEN_IMAGE = nil
                MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_CLOSED_IMAGE = nil
                MAWWW_FLOATING_HAIR_LAYER_IMAGE = nil
                MAWWW_FLOATING_BODY_LAYER_IMAGE = nil
            end
        else
            -- Keep the prior Roblox asset as a safe fallback on executors without local assets.
            MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_CLOSED_IMAGE = MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_IMAGE
            MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_OPEN_IMAGE = nil
            MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_CLOSED_IMAGE = nil
            MAWWW_FLOATING_HAIR_LAYER_IMAGE = nil
            MAWWW_FLOATING_BODY_LAYER_IMAGE = nil
        end
    end

                local left = TweenService:Create(launcherHeadPivot,
                    TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Rotation = -2.2})
                left:Play(); left.Completed:Wait()
                if not launcher.Parent or not screenGui.Parent then break end
                local right = TweenService:Create(launcherHeadPivot,
                    TweenInfo.new(0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Rotation = 2.2})
                right:Play(); right.Completed:Wait()
                if not launcher.Parent or not screenGui.Parent then break end
                local settle = TweenService:Create(launcherHeadPivot,
                    TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {Rotation = 0})
                settle:Play(); settle.Completed:Wait()
                task.wait(0.12)
            else
                task.wait(0.35)
            end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while launcherArt.Parent and screenGui.Parent do
            task.wait(2)
            if not launcherArt.Parent or not screenGui.Parent then break end
            launcherArt.Image = MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_CLOSED_IMAGE or MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_IMAGE or LOGO
            if launcherHeadArt then launcherHeadArt.Image = MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_CLOSED_IMAGE or MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_OPEN_IMAGE end
            task.wait(0.12)
            if not launcherArt.Parent or not screenGui.Parent then break end
            launcherArt.Image = MAWWW_FLOATING_LOGO_IMAGE or LOGO
            if launcherHeadArt then launcherHeadArt.Image = MAWWW_FLOATING_HEAD_OPEN_IMAGE end
        end
    end)

    -- Drag hanya menggeser jendela UI; tidak memengaruhi kontrol Auto Farm.
    local dragging = false
    local dragInput = nil
    local dragStart = nil
    local startPosition = nil
    local uiConnections = {}
    local function keepConnection(connection)
        if connection then table.insert(uiConnections, connection) end
        return connection
    end
    keepConnection(header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPosition = main.Position
            dragInput = input
        end
    end))
    keepConnection(header.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
            dragInput = nil
        end
    end))
    keepConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not dragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if dragInput and dragStart and startPosition then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                main.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPosition.X.Scale, startPosition.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPosition.Y.Scale, startPosition.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
            end
        end
    end))
    keepConnection(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = false
            dragInput = nil
        end
    end))

    local function hideWindow()
        main.Visible = false
        launcher.Visible = true
    end
    local function showWindow()
        main.Visible = true
        launcher.Visible = false
    end
    minimize.Activated:Connect(hideWindow)
    close.Activated:Connect(hideWindow)

    local launcherDragging, launcherMoved, launcherInput = false, false, nil
    local launcherPointerStart, launcherPositionStart = nil, nil
    local launcherDragThreshold = 3
    launcher.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if not launcher.Visible or launcherDragging then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        launcherDragging = true
        launcherMoved = false
        launcherInput = input
        launcherPointerStart = input.Position
        launcherPositionStart = launcher.Position
    end)
    keepConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if not launcherDragging or not launcherPointerStart or not launcherPositionStart
            or not launcher.Parent or not launcher.Visible then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
            and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local delta = input.Position - launcherPointerStart
        if delta.Magnitude >= launcherDragThreshold then launcherMoved = true end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local view = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(360, 720)
        local x = launcherPositionStart.X.Scale * view.X + launcherPositionStart.X.Offset + delta.X
        local y = launcherPositionStart.Y.Scale * view.Y + launcherPositionStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y
        x = math.clamp(x, 0, math.max(0, view.X - launcher.AbsoluteSize.X))
        y = math.clamp(y, 0, math.max(0, view.Y - launcher.AbsoluteSize.Y))
        launcher.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
    end))
    keepConnection(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if not launcherDragging or not launcherInput or not input then return end
        local sameMouse = launcherInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        local sameTouch = launcherInput.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
            and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
        if not sameMouse and not sameTouch then return end
        local shouldOpen = not launcherMoved
        launcherDragging, launcherMoved, launcherInput = false, false, nil
        launcherPointerStart, launcherPositionStart = nil, nil
        if shouldOpen then showWindow() end
    end))

    local function addSection(sectionTitle, detail)
        local frame = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, detail and 42 or 29),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Panel,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ZIndex = 8,
        }, body)
        corner(frame, 7)
        makeLabel(frame, sectionTitle, 11, UDim2.fromOffset(9, 1),
            UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, detail and 20 or 27), Enum.Font.GothamBold, COLORS.Cyan, 9)
        if detail then
            makeLabel(frame, detail, 8, UDim2.fromOffset(9, 20),
                UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 17), Enum.Font.Gotham, COLORS.SubText, 9)
        end
        return frame
    end

    local function addToggle(labelText, description, initialValue, callback)
        local row = new("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, description and 48 or 40),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Panel2,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "",
            AutoButtonColor = false,
            ZIndex = 8,
        }, body)
        corner(row, 8)
        local label = makeLabel(row, labelText, 10,
            UDim2.fromOffset(9, description and 4 or 2), UDim2.new(1, -94, 0, 19),
            Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, COLORS.Text, 9)
        if description then
            local desc = makeLabel(row, description, 8,
                UDim2.fromOffset(9, 23), UDim2.new(1, -94, 0, 19),
                Enum.Font.Gotham, COLORS.SubText, 9)
            desc.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        end
        local pill = new("TextLabel", {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(55, 24),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0.5, -12),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Panel3,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = "OFF",
            TextColor3 = COLORS.SubText,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
            TextSize = 9,
            ZIndex = 10,
        }, row)
        corner(pill, 7)
        local value = initialValue == true
        local function paint()
            pill.Text = value and "ON" or "OFF"
            pill.BackgroundColor3 = value and COLORS.Green or COLORS.Panel3
            pill.TextColor3 = value and Color3.fromRGB(11, 27, 24) or COLORS.SubText
            row.BackgroundColor3 = value and Color3.fromRGB(29, 48, 57) or COLORS.Panel2
        end
        local function update(nextValue, callCallback)
            value = nextValue == true
            paint()
            if callCallback ~= false and type(callback) == "function" then
                local ok, err = pcall(callback, value)
                if not ok then warn("[MAWWW Auto Farms UI] " .. labelText .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
            end
        end
        row.Activated:Connect(function() update(not value, true) end)
        paint()
        return { Set = update, Get = function() return value end, Row = row }
    end

    local function addSlider(labelText, minValue, maxValue, defaultValue, step, callback)
        local row = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 55),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Panel2,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ZIndex = 8,
        }, body)
        corner(row, 8)
        makeLabel(row, labelText, 10,
            UDim2.fromOffset(9, 2), UDim2.new(1, -75, 0, 18), Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, COLORS.Text, 9)
        local valueLabel = makeLabel(row, tostring(defaultValue), 10,
            UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 2), UDim2.fromOffset(52, 18), Enum.Font.GothamBold, COLORS.Cyan, 10)
        valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
        local track = new("Frame", {
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 31),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 12),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Panel3,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Active = true,
            ZIndex = 9,
        }, row)
        corner(track, 6)
        local fill = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Cyan,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ZIndex = 10,
        }, track)
        corner(fill, 6)
        local knob = new("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Text,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ZIndex = 11,
        }, track)
        corner(knob, 7)
        local value = defaultValue
        local draggingSlider = false
        local function snap(v)
            v = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or defaultValue, minValue, maxValue)
            local increment = step or 1
            v = minValue + math.floor(((v - minValue) / increment) + 0.5) * increment
            return math.clamp(v, minValue, maxValue)
        end
        local function paint()
            local alpha = (maxValue == minValue) and 0 or (value - minValue) / (maxValue - minValue)
            alpha = math.clamp(alpha, 0, 1)
            fill.Size = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 1, 0)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 0.5, 0)
            valueLabel.Text = tostring(value)
        end
        local function setValue(newValue, callCallback)
            value = snap(newValue)
            paint()
            if callCallback ~= false and type(callback) == "function" then
                local ok, err = pcall(callback, value)
                if not ok then warn("[MAWWW Auto Farms UI] Slider " .. labelText .. ": " .. tostring(err)) end
            end
        end
        local function setFromX(x)
            local width = track.AbsoluteSize.X
            if width <= 0 then return end
            local alpha = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / width, 0, 1)
            setValue(minValue + alpha * (maxValue - minValue), true)
        end
        track.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                draggingSlider = true
                setFromX(input.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        keepConnection(UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if draggingSlider and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                setFromX(input.Position.X)
            end
        end))
        keepConnection(UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                draggingSlider = false
            end
        end))
        paint()
        return { Set = setValue, Get = function() return value end }
    end

    local function addAction(labelText, callback, emphasis)
        local button = new("TextButton", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 38),
            BackgroundColor3 = emphasis and Color3.fromRGB(25, 74, 96) or COLORS.Panel2,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = labelText,
            TextColor3 = emphasis and COLORS.Cyan or COLORS.Text,
            Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold,
            TextSize = 10,
            AutoButtonColor = true,
            ZIndex = 8,
        }, body)
        corner(button, 8)
        button.Activated:Connect(function()
            local ok, err = pcall(callback)
            if not ok then
                warn("[MAWWW Auto Farms UI] " .. labelText .. ": " .. tostring(err))
                ForceNotify("Auto Farms", "Aksi gagal: " .. labelText, 3)
            end
        end)
        return button
    end

    local function addTextInput(labelText, placeholder, defaultText, onSubmit)
        local frame = new("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 62),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Panel2,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            ZIndex = 8,
        }, body)
        corner(frame, 8)
        makeLabel(frame, labelText, 10, UDim2.fromOffset(9, 2),
            UDim2.new(1, -18, 0, 18), Enum.Font.GothamSemibold, COLORS.Text, 9)
        local box = new("TextBox", {
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 25),
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 29),
            BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.Background,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Text = tostring(defaultText or ""),
            PlaceholderText = placeholder or "",
            PlaceholderColor3 = COLORS.SubText,
            TextColor3 = COLORS.Text,
            ClearTextOnFocus = false,
            TextSize = 9,
            Font = Enum.Font.Code,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            ZIndex = 10,
        }, frame)
        corner(box, 6)
        new("UIPadding", {
            PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 7),
            PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 7),
        }, box)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
            if type(onSubmit) == "function" then
                local ok, err = pcall(onSubmit, box.Text, enterPressed)
                if not ok then warn("[MAWWW Auto Farms UI] Input error: " .. tostring(err)) end
            end
        end)
        return box
    end

    local settingControls = {}

        function(v) AF.SetAutoFarm(v) end)
    settingControls.AutoHopAfterEscape = addToggle("Auto Hop After Escape", "Pindah server setelah escape berhasil.", AF.AutoHopAfterEscape,

    end)

        AF.SetMaxPlayers(v)
        if minSlider then minSlider.Set(AF.MinPlayers, false) end
        if maxSlider then maxSlider.Set(AF.MaxPlayers, false) end
    end)
    settingControls.MaxPlayers = maxSlider
    settingControls.AutoExecuteOnHop = addToggle("Auto Execute On Hop", "Queue loader MAWWW HUB saat pindah server.", AF.AutoExecuteOnHop,
        function(v) AF.SetAutoExecuteOnHop(v) end)

    addSection("SERVER CONTROLS", "Hop manual dan pengaturan daftar server")

        task.spawn(function() AF.HopNow("Manual hop") end)
    end, true)
    addAction("REJOIN THIS SERVER", function()
        TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, LocalPlayer)
    end, false)
    addAction("RESET HOP BLACKLIST", function()
        AF.ResetServerBlacklist()
        ForceNotify("Auto Farms", "Daftar blacklist server direset.", 3)
    end, false)

    addSection("AWARD WEBHOOK", "Opsional: laporan statistik setelah escape")
    local webhookInput = addTextInput("Discord Webhook URL", "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/...", AF.WebhookURL or "",
        function(value)
            AF.WebhookURL = tostring(value or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        end)
    settingControls.AwardWebhookEnabled = addToggle("Award Status", "Laporan Screws, Gears, EXP, dan Level.", AF.AwardWebhookEnabled,
        function(v) AF.SetAwardWebhook(v) end)
    addAction("TEST WEBHOOK", function()
        AF.WebhookURL = tostring(webhookInput.Text or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        ForceNotify("Award Webhook", "Laporan statistik dikirim setelah Escape Gate berhasil.", 3)
    end, false)

    --==============================================================
    -- CONFIG SAVE / LOAD / AUTO-LOAD
    -- Config only stores safe UI settings; webhook URLs are intentionally
    -- excluded so private webhook credentials are not written to disk.
    --==============================================================
    local AF_CONFIG_FOLDER = "MAWWW"
    local AF_CONFIG_PATH = AF_CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/autofarms_config.json"
    local AF_AUTOLOAD_PATH = AF_CONFIG_FOLDER .. "/autofarms_autoload.txt"
    local AF_CONFIG_VERSION = 2

    local function AF_EnsureConfigFolder()
        if type(makefolder) == "function" then
            local exists = false
            if type(isfolder) == "function" then
                pcall(function() exists = isfolder(AF_CONFIG_FOLDER) end)
            end
            if not exists then
                pcall(function() makefolder(AF_CONFIG_FOLDER) end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function AF_ReadAutoLoadPreference()
        if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return false end
        local ok, raw = pcall(function() return readfile(AF_AUTOLOAD_PATH) end)
        return ok and type(raw) == "string" and raw:lower():match("^%s*true%s*$") ~= nil
    end

    -- Auto Load selalu ON sebagai default; simpan preferensinya agar bertahan pada execute berikutnya.
    local AF_AutoLoadConfig = true

    local function AF_WriteAutoLoadPreference()
        AF_EnsureConfigFolder()
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" then return false end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            writefile(AF_AUTOLOAD_PATH, AF_AutoLoadConfig and "true" or "false")
        end)
        return ok
    end
    AF_WriteAutoLoadPreference()

    local function AF_GetConfigSettings()
        return {
            AutoFarm = AF.AutoFarm == true,
            AutoHopAfterEscape = AF.AutoHopAfterEscape == true,
            DisableKillerChance = AF.DisableKillerChance == true,
            HopIfKiller = AF.HopIfKiller == true,
            InstantFinish = AF.InstantFinish == true,
            GateTweenSpeed = tonumber(AF.GateTweenSpeed) or 100,
            AntiAFK = AF.AntiAFK == true,
            NoClip = AF.NoClip == true,
            MinPlayers = tonumber(AF.MinPlayers) or 2,
            MaxPlayers = tonumber(AF.MaxPlayers) or 4,
            AutoExecuteOnHop = AF.AutoExecuteOnHop == true,
            AwardWebhookEnabled = AF.AwardWebhookEnabled == true,
            Invisible = W.Invisible_IsOn() == true,
        }
    end

    local function AF_SaveConfig(silent)
        if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
            if not silent then ForceNotify("Config", "Executor tidak menyediakan writefile; config tidak bisa disimpan.", 4) end
            return false
        end
        AF_EnsureConfigFolder()
        local payload = {
            version = AF_CONFIG_VERSION,
            savedAt = os.time(),
            settings = AF_GetConfigSettings(),
        }
        local encodeOK, encoded = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(payload) end)
        if not encodeOK or type(encoded) ~= "string" then
            if not silent then ForceNotify("Config", "Gagal mengubah config ke JSON.", 4) end
            return false
        end
        local writeOK, writeError = pcall(function() writefile(AF_CONFIG_PATH, encoded) end)
        if not writeOK then
            if not silent then ForceNotify("Config", "Gagal menyimpan config: " .. tostring(writeError), 4) end
            return false
        end
        if not silent then ForceNotify("Config", "Config berhasil disimpan.", 3) end
        return true
    end

    local function AF_LoadConfig(silent)
        if type(readfile) ~= "function" then
            if not silent then ForceNotify("Config", "Executor tidak menyediakan readfile; config tidak bisa dimuat.", 4) end
            return false
        end
        local readOK, raw = pcall(function() return readfile(AF_CONFIG_PATH) end)
        if not readOK or type(raw) ~= "string" or raw == "" then
            if not silent then ForceNotify("Config", "Config belum ada. Tekan SAVE CONFIG terlebih dahulu.", 4) end
            return false
        end
        local decodeOK, payload = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(raw) end)
        if not decodeOK or type(payload) ~= "table" then
            if not silent then ForceNotify("Config", "File config rusak atau JSON tidak valid.", 4) end
            return false
        end
        -- Versi 1 menyimpan default lama (beberapa toggle OFF dan Max Players 4).
        -- Abaikan sekali, lalu tulis config versi baru dengan default yang diminta.
        if tonumber(payload.version) ~= AF_CONFIG_VERSION then
            return false
        end
        local settings = type(payload.settings) == "table" and payload.settings or payload

        local booleanKeys = {
            "AutoFarm", "AutoHopAfterEscape",
            "DisableKillerChance", "HopIfKiller", "InstantFinish", "AntiAFK", "NoClip",
            "AutoExecuteOnHop", "AwardWebhookEnabled", "Invisible",
        }
        for _, key in ipairs(booleanKeys) do
            if type(settings[key]) == "boolean" and settingControls[key] then
                settingControls[key].Set(settings[key], true)
            end
        end
        -- The invisible toggle must update both the saved UI state and the runtime API state.
        -- forceApply prevents the short toggle debounce from ignoring an immediately loaded config.
        if type(settings.Invisible) == "boolean" and settingControls.Invisible then
            W.Invisible_SetState(settings.Invisible, true, true)
        end
        if type(settings.GateTweenSpeed) == "number" and settingControls.GateTweenSpeed then
            settingControls.GateTweenSpeed.Set(settings.GateTweenSpeed, true)
        end

        -- Apply minimum first so the paired sliders stay in a valid range.
        if type(settings.MinPlayers) == "number" and settingControls.MinPlayers then
            settingControls.MinPlayers.Set(settings.MinPlayers, true)
        end
        if type(settings.MaxPlayers) == "number" and settingControls.MaxPlayers then
            settingControls.MaxPlayers.Set(settings.MaxPlayers, true)
        end
        if settingControls.MinPlayers then settingControls.MinPlayers.Set(AF.MinPlayers, false) end
        if settingControls.MaxPlayers then settingControls.MaxPlayers.Set(AF.MaxPlayers, false) end

        if not silent then ForceNotify("Config", "Config berhasil dimuat.", 3) end
        return true
    end

    addSection("CONFIGURATION", "Save, load, dan terapkan config saat script dijalankan")
    settingControls.AutoLoadConfig = addToggle("Auto Load Config", "Otomatis memuat config saat script selesai di-execute.", AF_AutoLoadConfig,
        function(value)
            AF_AutoLoadConfig = value == true
            local persisted = AF_WriteAutoLoadPreference()
            if not persisted then
                ForceNotify("Config", "Pilihan Auto Load hanya berlaku pada sesi ini; executor tidak mendukung penyimpanan file.", 4)
            else
                ForceNotify("Config", AF_AutoLoadConfig and "Auto Load Config diaktifkan." or "Auto Load Config dinonaktifkan.", 3)
            end
        end)
    addAction("SAVE CONFIG", function() AF_SaveConfig(false) end, true)
    addAction("LOAD CONFIG", function() AF_LoadConfig(false) end, false)

    -- Automatically load once controls are fully constructed. If no current config exists,
    -- activate every toggle by callback, then save these defaults for the next execution.
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.35)
        if AF_AutoLoadConfig and screenGui.Parent and AF.Alive then
            local loaded = AF_LoadConfig(true)
            if not loaded then
                local defaultOnKeys = {
                    "AutoFarm", "AutoHopAfterEscape", "DisableKillerChance", "HopIfKiller",
                    "InstantFinish", "AntiAFK", "NoClip", "Invisible",
                    "AutoExecuteOnHop", "AwardWebhookEnabled", "AutoLoadConfig",
                }
                for _, key in ipairs(defaultOnKeys) do
                    local control = settingControls[key]
                    if control then control.Set(true, true) end
                end
                AF.SetMinPlayers(2)
                AF.SetMaxPlayers(2)
                if settingControls.MinPlayers then settingControls.MinPlayers.Set(2, false) end
                if settingControls.MaxPlayers then settingControls.MaxPlayers.Set(2, false) end
                AF_SaveConfig(true)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Hubungkan status footer ke engine; jendela dapat disembunyikan tanpa menghentikan farm.
    task.spawn(function()
        while screenGui.Parent and AF.Alive and W.N3K0_AutoFarm == AF do
            local role = "Unknown"
            pcall(function() role = AF.GetRoleName and AF.GetRoleName() or "Unknown" end)
            statusLabel.Text = "Status: " .. tostring(AF.Status or "Idle") .. "  |  Role: " .. tostring(role)
            statusLabel.TextColor3 = AF.AutoFarm and COLORS.Green or COLORS.SubText
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)

    __afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_UI_CLEANUP = function()
        for _, connection in ipairs(uiConnections) do
            pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
        end
        table.clear(uiConnections)
        pcall(function() if screenGui and screenGui.Parent then screenGui:Destroy() end end)
    end
    __afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_UI = screenGui
    __afEnv.MAWWW_HUB_AUTOFARMS_INSTANCE = AF

    ForceNotify("MAWWW Auto Farms", "Auto Farms berhasil dimuat.", 3)
end

end

-- Start the existing Auto Farms runtime directly (no key gate).
local __mawwwStartOK, __mawwwStartError = pcall(function()
    MAWWW_StartAutoFarms()
end)
if not __mawwwStartOK then
    warn("[MAWWW] Auto Farms startup error: " .. tostring(__mawwwStartError))
end
